This form discloses their equity holdings.\n\n## Data Structure\n\n### COVERPAGE.tsv\nContains general information about the filing entity.\n\n**Key Fields:**\n- `ACCESSION_NUMBER`: Unique identifier for the filing\n- `REPORTCALENDARORQUARTER`: Quarter end date (e.g., \"30-SEP-2025\")\n- `FILINGMANAGER_NAME`: Name of the fund/manager\n- `FORM13FFILENUMBER`: SEC file number\n- `CRDNUMBER`: CRD number\n\n**Example:**\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\n# Search for a fund\nmatches = coverpage[coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n    'renaissance technologies', case=False, na=False\n)]\n```\n\n### SUBMISSION.tsv\nContains submission metadata linking filings to entities.\n\n**Key Fields:**\n- `ACCESSION_NUMBER`: Unique identifier\n- `CIK`: Central Index Key (company identifier)\n- `PERIODOFREPORT`: Reporting period\n- `FILING_DATE`: Date filed with SEC\n\n### SUMMARYPAGE.tsv\nContains summary information including AUM.\n\n**Key Fields:**\n- `ACCESSION_NUMBER`: Links to the filing\n- `OTHERINCLUDEDMANAGERSCOUNT`: Number of other managers\n- `TABLEENTRYTOTAL`: Total number of holdings\n- `TABLEVALUETOTAL`: Total value of holdings (in thousands)\n\n**Important:** `TABLEVALUETOTAL` is the AUM in thousands of dollars.\n\n**Example:**\n```python\nsummary = pd.read_csv('SUMMARYPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nfund_summary = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\naum = fund_summary.iloc[0]['TABLEVALUETOTAL'] * 1000  # Convert to dollars\n```\n\n### INFOTABLE.tsv\nContains detailed holdings information for each security.\n\n**Key Fields:**\n- `ACCESSION_NUMBER`: Links to the filing\n- `NAMEOFISSUER`: Company name\n- `CUSIP`: Security identifier (9 characters)\n- `VALUE`: Market value in thousands of dollars\n- `SSHPRNAMT`: Number of shares/principal amount\n- `SSHPRNAMTTYPE`: Type (SH=shares, PRN=principal)\n- `VOTING_AUTH_SOLE`: Sole voting authority\n- `VOTING_AUTH_SHARED`: Shared voting authority\n\n**Example:**\n```python\nholdings = pd.read_csv('INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nfund_holdings = holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n\n# Count number of stocks\nnum_stocks = len(fund_holdings)\n\n# Find specific security\npalantir = holdings[holdings['CUSIP'] == '69608A108']\n```\n\n## Common Analysis Workflows\n\n### 1. Count Holdings for a Fund\n```python\nholdings = pd.read_csv('Q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nfund_holdings = holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession]\nnum_stocks = len(fund_holdings)\n```\n\n### 3. Compare Holdings Between Quarters\n```python\n# Get accession numbers for both quarters\nq2_accession = get_accession('2025-q2', 'berkshire hathaway')\nq3_accession = get_accession('2025-q3', 'berkshire hathaway')\n\n# Load holdings\nq2_holdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q2/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nq3_holdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t')\n\n# Filter by fund\nq2_fund = q2_holdings[q2_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession]\nq3_fund = q3_holdings[q3_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession]\n\n# Merge on CUSIP\nmerged = pd.merge(\n    q2_fund[['CUSIP', 'VALUE']],\n    q3_fund[['CUSIP', 'VALUE']],\n    on='CUSIP',\n    how='outer',\n    suffixes=('_q2', '_q3')\n)\n\n# Fill NaN with 0 for new/closed positions\nmerged = merged.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate change\nmerged['change'] = merged['VALUE_q3'] - merged['VALUE_q2']\n\n# Get top 5 increases\ntop_5 = merged.nlargest(5, 'change')\n```\n\n### 4. Find Top Holders of a Security\n```python\n# Search for security by name or CUSIP\nholdings = pd.read_csv('Q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t')\npalantir_holdings = holdings[holdings['NAMEOFISSUER'].str.contains(\n    'PALANTIR', case=False, na=False\n)]\n\n# Get top holders by value\ntop_holders = palantir_holdings.nlargest(3, 'VALUE')\n\n# Map back to fund names\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('Q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nresult = pd.merge(\n    top_holders,\n    coverpage[['ACCESSION_NUMBER', 'FILINGMANAGER_NAME']],\n    on='ACCESSION_NUMBER'\n)\n```\n\n## Important Notes\n\n1. **Value Units:** All VALUE fields are in thousands of dollars\n2. **CUSIP:** 9-character identifier for securities\n3. **Accession Numbers:** Change each quarter for the same fund\n4. **Fuzzy Matching:** Fund names may vary slightly, use case-insensitive partial matching\n5. **Data Quality:** Some fields may be missing or contain unexpected values\n\n## Common Fund Names to Watch\n\n- Renaissance Technologies (Jim Simons)\n- Berkshire Hathaway (Warren Buffett)\n- Bridgewater Associates (Ray Dalio)\n- BlackRock\n- Vanguard Group\n- Citadel Advisors\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1153,"cache_read_input_tokens":20551,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1153,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b4cbe571-2119-46a4-b945-e5e265aa1af0","uuid":"845feb0b-b2d8-4a5e-8612-6063c21114f9"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01Ur3VzRkqBM5U4cAQ86pSWD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_018RARHaVKoAzBbuoKqGFUhk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/python-fuzzy-string-matching.md","content":"# Python Fuzzy String Matching\n\n## Overview\nFuzzy string matching allows finding similar strings even when they don't match exactly. Useful for searching fund names, company names, and other text data that may have variations.\n\n## Methods\n\n### 1. Simple Case-Insensitive Substring Matching\n\nThe simplest approach using pandas:\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndf = pd.read_csv('data.tsv', sep='\\t')\n\n# Case-insensitive contains\nmatches = df[df['name'].str.contains('search term', case=False, na=False)]\n\n# Get best match (first one)\nbest_match = matches.iloc[0]\n```\n\n**Pros:** Fast, simple, built-in\n**Cons:** No ranking by similarity\n\n### 2. Regular Expressions\n\nMore flexible pattern matching:\n\n```python\nimport re\n\n# Create pattern\npattern = re.compile(r'renaissance.*technologies', re.IGNORECASE)\n\n# Filter dataframe\nmatches = df[df['name'].str.contains(pattern, na=False)]\n```\n\n**Pros:** Flexible, powerful\n**Cons:** Requires regex knowledge\n\n### 3. FuzzyWuzzy / RapidFuzz\n\nAdvanced similarity scoring:\n\n```bash\npip install rapidfuzz\n```\n\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz, process\n\n# Simple ratio\nscore = fuzz.ratio(\"renaissance technologies\", \"Renaissance Technologies LLC\")\n# Returns: 76\n\n# Partial ratio (substring matching)\nscore = fuzz.partial_ratio(\"renaissance\", \"Renaissance Technologies LLC\")\n# Returns: 100\n\n# Find best match in a list\nchoices = df['name'].tolist()\nbest_match = process.extractOne(\"renaissance technologies\", choices)\n# Returns: ('Renaissance Technologies LLC', 95.0, index)\n\n# Find top N matches\ntop_matches = process.extract(\"renaissance technologies\", choices, limit=3)\n```\n\n**Matching Functions:**\n- `fuzz.ratio()`: Simple character-by-character comparison\n- `fuzz.partial_ratio()`: Best matching substring\n- `fuzz.token_sort_ratio()`: Ignores word order\n- `fuzz.token_set_ratio()`: Ignores word order and duplicates\n\n**Pros:** Sophisticated matching, ranked results\n**Cons:** Requires extra library, slower on large datasets\n\n### 4. Combining with Pandas\n\n```python\nfrom rapidfuzz import fuzz\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef find_best_match(df, column, search_term, threshold=80):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find best matching row using fuzzy matching.\n\n    Args:\n        df: DataFrame to search\n        column: Column name to search in\n        search_term: Term to search for\n        threshold: Minimum similarity score (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n        Best matching row or None\n    \"\"\"\n    scores = df[column].apply(\n        lambda x: fuzz.partial_ratio(search_term.lower(), str(x).lower())\n    )\n\n    best_idx = scores.idxmax()\n    best_score = scores[best_idx]\n\n    if best_score >= threshold:\n        return df.loc[best_idx]\n    return None\n\n# Usage\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nmatch = find_best_match(\n    coverpage,\n    'FILINGMANAGER_NAME',\n    'renaissance technologies',\n    threshold=70\n)\n```\n\n## Use Cases in Finance\n\n### Finding Fund by Name\n```python\n# Variations in fund names:\n# - \"Renaissance Technologies LLC\"\n# - \"RENAISSANCE TECHNOLOGIES CORP\"\n# - \"Renaissance Tech\"\n\nsearch_term = \"renaissance technologies\"\nmatches = coverpage[coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n    search_term, case=False, na=False\n)]\n\n# If multiple matches, could rank by fuzzy score\nif len(matches) > 1:\n    from rapidfuzz import fuzz\n    matches['score'] = matches['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].apply(\n        lambda x: fuzz.token_set_ratio(search_term, x)\n    )\n    best_match = matches.nlargest(1, 'score').iloc[0]\n```\n\n### Finding Securities\n```python\n# Find \"Palantir Technologies\" which might appear as:\n# - \"PALANTIR TECHNOLOGIES INC\"\n# - \"Palantir Technologies Inc COM\"\n# - \"PALANTIR TECH INC\"\n\nsearch_term = \"palantir\"\nmatches = holdings[holdings['NAMEOFISSUER'].str.contains(\n    search_term, case=False, na=False\n)]\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Start Simple:** Try case-insensitive substring matching first\n2. **Normalize Input:** Convert to lowercase, strip whitespace\n3. **Choose Right Method:**\n   - Exact matching → `==`\n   - Substring → `.str.contains()`\n   - Fuzzy → `rapidfuzz`\n4. **Set Thresholds:** For fuzzy matching, use ~70-80% threshold\n5. **Validate Results:** Check that your matches make sense\n6. **Handle Multiple Matches:** Decide how to pick best one\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- **False Positives:** \"Bank of America\" might match \"America First Bank\"\n- **Performance:** Fuzzy matching on large datasets can be slow\n- **Encoding Issues:** Ensure proper string encoding (UTF-8)\n- **None/NaN Values:** Use `na=False` in pandas contains\n\n## Quick Reference\n\n```python\n# Simple exact match\ndf[df['name'] == 'exact name']\n\n# Case-insensitive substring\ndf[df['name'].str.contains('term', case=False, na=False)]\n\n# Multiple terms (OR)\ndf[df['name'].str.contains('term1|term2', case=False, na=False)]\n\n# Multiple terms (AND)\nmask = (df['name'].str.contains('term1', case=False, na=False) &\n        df['name'].str.contains('term2', case=False, na=False))\ndf[mask]\n\n# Fuzzy with ranking\nfrom rapidfuzz import process\nbest = process.extractOne('search', df['name'].tolist())\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1940,"cache_read_input_tokens":21704,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1940,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b4cbe571-2119-46a4-b945-e5e265aa1af0","uuid":"7680fcf8-2b14-4746-a952-1c0337b54154"}

Case-insensitive search\n    matches = coverpage[coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n        search_term, case=False, na=False\n    )]\n\n    if len(matches) == 0:\n        print(f\"No matches found for '{search_term}'\")\n        return None\n\n    if len(matches) > 1:\n        print(f\"Multiple matches found for '{search_term}':\")\n        for idx, row in matches.iterrows():\n            print(f\"  - {row['FILINGMANAGER_NAME']}\")\n        print(f\"Using first match: {matches.iloc[0]['FILINGMANAGER_NAME']}\")\n\n    return matches.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n\ndef get_fund_aum(summary_path, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"\n    Get the AUM for a fund from SUMMARYPAGE.\n\n    Args:\n        summary_path: Path to SUMMARYPAGE.tsv file\n        accession_number: Accession number of the fund\n\n    Returns:\n        AUM in dollars (float)\n    \"\"\"\n    summary = pd.read_csv(summary_path, sep='\\t')\n    fund_summary = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n\n    if len(fund_summary) == 0:\n        print(f\"No summary found for accession {accession_number}\")\n        return None\n\n    # TABLEVALUETOTAL is in thousands\n    aum = fund_summary.iloc[0]['TABLEVALUETOTAL'] * 1000\n    return aum\n\n\ndef count_fund_holdings(infotable_path, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"\n    Count the number of stocks held by a fund.\n\n    Args:\n        infotable_path: Path to INFOTABLE.tsv file\n        accession_number: Accession number of the fund\n\n    Returns:\n        Number of stocks (int)\n    \"\"\"\n    holdings = pd.read_csv(infotable_path, sep='\\t')\n    fund_holdings = holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    return len(fund_holdings)\n\n\ndef compare_fund_holdings(q2_infotable, q3_infotable, q2_accession, q3_accession, top_n=5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compare holdings between Q2 and Q3 to find top increased positions.\n\n    Args:\n        q2_infotable: Path to Q2 INFOTABLE.tsv\n        q3_infotable: Path to Q3 INFOTABLE.tsv\n        q2_accession: Q2 accession number\n        q3_accession: Q3 accession number\n        top_n: Number of top positions to return\n\n    Returns:\n        List of CUSIPs with largest value increases\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load holdings\n    q2_holdings = pd.read_csv(q2_infotable, sep='\\t')\n    q3_holdings = pd.read_csv(q3_infotable, sep='\\t')\n\n    # Filter by fund\n    q2_fund = q2_holdings[q2_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession][['CUSIP', 'VALUE', 'NAMEOFISSUER']]\n    q3_fund = q3_holdings[q3_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession][['CUSIP', 'VALUE', 'NAMEOFISSUER']]\n\n    # Merge on CUSIP\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        q2_fund,\n        q3_fund,\n        on='CUSIP',\n        how='outer',\n        suffixes=('_q2', '_q3')\n    )\n\n    # Fill NaN with 0 (for new positions or closed positions)\n    merged['VALUE_q2'] = merged['VALUE_q2'].fillna(0)\n    merged['VALUE_q3'] = merged['VALUE_q3'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Calculate change (in thousands of dollars)\n    merged['value_change'] = merged['VALUE_q3'] - merged['VALUE_q2']\n\n    # Get top N increases\n    top_increases = merged.nlargest(top_n, 'value_change')\n\n    print(f\"\\nTop {top_n} increased positions:\")\n    for idx, row in top_increases.iterrows():\n        name = row['NAMEOFISSUER_q3'] if pd.notna(row['NAMEOFISSUER_q3']) else row['NAMEOFISSUER_q2']\n        print(f\"  {row['CUSIP']}: {name}\")\n        print(f\"    Q2: ${row['VALUE_q2']:,.0f}k, Q3: ${row['VALUE_q3']:,.0f}k, Change: ${row['value_change']:,.0f}k\")\n\n    return top_increases['CUSIP'].tolist()\n\n\ndef find_top_holders_by_security(infotable_path, coverpage_path, search_term, top_n=3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find top holders of a security by searching for it.\n\n    Args:\n        infotable_path: Path to INFOTABLE.tsv\n        coverpage_path: Path to COVERPAGE.tsv\n        search_term: Search term for the security name\n        top_n: Number of top holders to return\n\n    Returns:\n        List of fund manager names\n    \"\"\"\n    # Get top holders by value\n    top_holders = matches.nlargest(top_n, 'VALUE')\n\n    # Load coverpage to get fund names\n    coverpage = pd.read_csv(coverpage_path, sep='\\t')\n\n    # Merge to get fund names\n    result = pd.merge(\n        top_holders,\n        coverpage[['ACCESSION_NUMBER', 'FILINGMANAGER_NAME']],\n        on='ACCESSION_NUMBER',\n        how='left'\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nTop {top_n} holders of {search_term}:\")\n    for idx, row in result.iterrows():\n        print(f\"  {row['FILINGMANAGER_NAME']}\")\n        print(f\"    Value: ${row['VALUE']:,.0f}k, CUSIP: {row['CUSIP']}\")\n\n    return result['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].tolist()\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main analysis function.\"\"\"\n\n    # Starting January 2023, TABLEVALUETOTAL is in dollars (not thousands)\n    aum = fund_summary.iloc[0]['TABLEVALUETOTAL']\n    return aum\n\n\ndef count_fund_holdings(infotable_path, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"\n    Count the number of stocks held by a fund.\n\n    Args:\n        infotable_path: Path to INFOTABLE.tsv file\n        accession_number: Accession number of the fund\n\n    Returns:\n        Number of stocks (int)\n    \"\"\"\n    holdings = pd.read_csv(infotable_path, sep='\\t')\n    fund_holdings = holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n    return len(fund_holdings)\n\n\ndef compare_fund_holdings(q2_infotable, q3_infotable, q2_accession, q3_accession, top_n=5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compare holdings between Q2 and Q3 to find top increased positions.\n\n    Args:\n        q2_infotable: Path to Q2 INFOTABLE.tsv\n        q3_infotable: Path to Q3 INFOTABLE.tsv\n        q2_accession: Q2 accession number\n        q3_accession: Q3 accession number\n        top_n: Number of top positions to return\n\n    Returns:\n        List of CUSIPs with largest value increases\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load holdings\n    q2_holdings = pd.read_csv(q2_infotable, sep='\\t')\n    q3_holdings = pd.read_csv(q3_infotable, sep='\\t')\n\n    # Filter by fund\n    q2_fund = q2_holdings[q2_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession][['CUSIP', 'VALUE', 'NAMEOFISSUER']]\n    q3_fund = q3_holdings[q3_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession][['CUSIP', 'VALUE', 'NAMEOFISSUER']]\n\n    # Merge on CUSIP\n    merged = pd.merge(\n        q2_fund,\n        q3_fund,\n        on='CUSIP',\n        how='outer',\n        suffixes=('_q2', '_q3')\n    )\n\n    # Fill NaN with 0 (for new positions or closed positions)\n    merged['VALUE_q2'] = merged['VALUE_q2'].fillna(0)\n    merged['VALUE_q3'] = merged['VALUE_q3'].fillna(0)\n\n    # Load holdings\n    holdings = pd.read_csv(infotable_path, sep='\\t')\n\n    # Search for security\n    matches = holdings[holdings['NAMEOFISSUER'].str.contains(\n        search_term, case=False, na=False\n    )]\n\n    if len(matches) == 0:\n        print(f\"No securities found matching '{search_term}'\")\n        return []\n\n    # Check CUSIP consistency\n    cusips = matches['CUSIP'].unique()\n    if len(cusips) > 1:\n        print(f\"Warning: Multiple CUSIPs found for '{search_term}': {cusips}\")\n        print(\"Using all matches\")\n\n    # Filter out amendments if requested\n    if exclude_amendments and 'ISAMENDMENT' in matches.columns:\n        matches = matches[matches['ISAMENDMENT'] != 'Y']\n\n    if len(matches) == 0:\n        print(f\"No matches found for '{search_term}'\")\n        return None\n\n    if len(matches) > 1:\n        print(f\"Multiple matches found for '{search_term}':\")\n        for idx, row in matches.iterrows():\n            print(f\"  - {row['FILINGMANAGER_NAME']}\")\n        print(f\"Using first match: {matches.iloc[0]['FILINGMANAGER_NAME']}\")\n\n    return matches.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n\ndef get_fund_aum(summary_path, accession_number):\n    \"\"\"\n    Get the AUM for a fund from SUMMARYPAGE.\n\n    Args:\n        summary_path: Path to SUMMARYPAGE.tsv file\n        accession_number: Accession number of the fund\n\n    Returns:\n        AUM in dollars (float)\n    \"\"\"\n    summary = pd.read_csv(summary_path, sep='\\t')\n    fund_summary = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession_number]\n\n    if len(fund_summary) == 0:\n        print(f\"No summary found for accession {accession_number}\")\n        return None\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_018gmyRjpGE6JMdwq6PrhpAS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01CuG4d144h7UACicnQv5iHw","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/hedge-fund-13f-analysis-workflow.md","content":"# Hedge Fund SEC Form 13F Analysis Workflow\n\n## Overview\nThis document provides a complete workflow for analyzing hedge fund activities using SEC Form 13F filings data in TSV format.\n\n## Dataset Structure\n\nForm 13F data is typically organized in quarterly folders (e.g., `2025-q2`, `2025-q3`) containing:\n- `COVERPAGE.tsv` - Fund manager information\n- `SUMMARYPAGE.tsv` - AUM and summary statistics\n- `INFOTABLE.tsv` - Detailed holdings (large file)\n- `SUBMISSION.tsv` - Filing metadata\n- Other supporting files\n\n## Common Analysis Tasks\n\n### 1. Find Fund AUM (Assets Under Management)\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Search COVERPAGE for fund name to get accession number\n2. Use accession number to query SUMMARYPAGE for AUM\n\n**Code:**\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Step 1: Find accession number\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nmatches = coverpage[coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n    'renaissance technologies', case=False, na=False\n)]\n# Filter out amendments\nmatches = matches[matches['ISAMENDMENT'] != 'Y']\naccession = matches.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n# Step 2: Get AUM\nsummary = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/SUMMARYPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nfund_summary = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession]\naum = fund_summary.iloc[0]['TABLEVALUETOTAL']  # In dollars since Jan 2023\n```\n\n### 2. Count Holdings for a Fund\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Get accession number (as above)\n2. Count rows in INFOTABLE for that accession\n\n**Code:**\n```python\nholdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\nfund_holdings = holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession]\nnum_stocks = len(fund_holdings)\n```\n\n### 3. Compare Holdings Between Quarters\n\n**Purpose:** Find which stocks a fund increased/decreased investment in\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Get accession numbers for both quarters\n2. Load holdings for both quarters\n3. Aggregate by CUSIP (to handle multiple entries per security)\n4. Merge and calculate differences\n5. Sort by change to find top increases/decreases\n\n**Code:**\n```python\n# Get accession numbers\nq2_accession = find_fund_accession('2025-q2/COVERPAGE.tsv', 'Berkshire Hathaway Inc')\nq3_accession = find_fund_accession('2025-q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', 'Berkshire Hathaway Inc')\n\n# Load holdings\nq2_holdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q2/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\nq3_holdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n\n# Filter and aggregate by CUSIP\nq2_fund = q2_holdings[q2_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession]\nq3_fund = q3_holdings[q3_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession]\n\nq2_agg = q2_fund.groupby('CUSIP').agg({\n    'VALUE': 'sum',\n    'NAMEOFISSUER': 'first'\n}).reset_index()\n\nq3_agg = q3_fund.groupby('CUSIP').agg({\n    'VALUE': 'sum',\n    'NAMEOFISSUER': 'first'\n}).reset_index()\n\n# Merge and calculate change\nmerged = pd.merge(q2_agg, q3_agg, on='CUSIP', how='outer', suffixes=('_q2', '_q3'))\nmerged['VALUE_q2'] = merged['VALUE_q2'].fillna(0)\nmerged['VALUE_q3'] = merged['VALUE_q3'].fillna(0)\nmerged['change'] = merged['VALUE_q3'] - merged['VALUE_q2']\n\n# Top 5 increases\ntop_increases = merged.nlargest(5, 'change')\ntop_cusips = top_increases['CUSIP'].tolist()\n```\n\n**Why Aggregation is Important:**\n- Funds may have multiple entries for the same CUSIP (different classes, options, etc.)\n- Must sum all entries before comparing quarters\n- Without aggregation, you'll get duplicate CUSIPs in results\n\n### 4. Find Top Holders of a Security\n\n**Purpose:** Identify which funds have the largest positions in a stock\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Search INFOTABLE for security name or CUSIP\n2. Sort by VALUE to find top holders\n3. Join with COVERPAGE to get fund manager names\n\n**Code:**\n```python\n# Search for security\nholdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\npalantir = holdings[holdings['NAMEOFISSUER'].str.contains(\n    'palantir', case=False, na=False\n)]\n\n# Get top holders\ntop_holders = palantir.nlargest(3, 'VALUE')\n\n# Add fund names\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nresult = pd.merge(\n    top_holders,\n    coverpage[['ACCESSION_NUMBER', 'FILINGMANAGER_NAME']],\n    on='ACCESSION_NUMBER'\n)\n\nfund_names = result['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].tolist()\n```\n\n## Important Considerations\n\n### Data Quality Issues\n\n1. **Multiple CUSIPs for Same Security**: Some securities may have variations in CUSIP due to data entry errors\n2. **Multiple Entries per CUSIP**: Funds may report the same CUSIP multiple times (different share classes, options)\n3. **Amendments**: Filter out amendment filings (`ISAMENDMENT != 'Y'`) to avoid duplicates\n4. **Missing Values**: Some fields may be NULL or empty\n\n### Performance Tips\n\n1. **Use `low_memory=False`**: Prevents dtype warnings on large files\n2. **Filter Early**: Filter by accession number before loading full datasets\n3. **Aggregate When Needed**: Always aggregate by CUSIP when comparing holdings\n4. **Use Specific Columns**: Select only needed columns to reduce memory usage\n\n### Value Units (CRITICAL)\n\n- **Before January 3, 2023**: Values in thousands of dollars\n- **After January 3, 2023**: Values in dollars\n\nFor Q2 and Q3 2025 data, all VALUE fields are in dollars (not thousands).\n\n## Complete Workflow Example\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\n\ndef analyze_hedge_funds(q2_dir, q3_dir):\n    \"\"\"Complete analysis workflow.\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n\n    # Q1: Renaissance AUM in Q3\n    coverpage = pd.read_csv(f'{q3_dir}/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\n    matches = coverpage[\n        (coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains('renaissance', case=False, na=False)) &\n        (coverpage['ISAMENDMENT'] != 'Y')\n    ]\n    accession = matches.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n    summary = pd.read_csv(f'{q3_dir}/SUMMARYPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\n    aum = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession].iloc[0]['TABLEVALUETOTAL']\n    results['q1_answer'] = int(aum)\n\n    # Q2: Count holdings\n    holdings = pd.read_csv(f'{q3_dir}/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n    count = len(holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession])\n    results['q2_answer'] = count\n\n    # Q3: Berkshire increases\n    # (See \"Compare Holdings Between Quarters\" section above)\n\n    # Q4: Palantir top holders\n    # (See \"Find Top Holders of a Security\" section above)\n\n    return results\n\n# Save results\nwith open('answers.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(results, f, indent=4)\n```\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n1. **Not filtering amendments**: Results in duplicate data\n2. **Not aggregating by CUSIP**: Creates cartesian product on merge\n3. **Wrong value units**: Remember values are in dollars (post-2023)\n4. **Case sensitivity**: Always use `case=False` for string searches\n5. **NaN handling**: Fill NaN with 0 before calculations\n\n## Testing Your Results\n\nVerify your analysis by:\n1. Checking AUM against public sources (if available)\n2. Ensuring no duplicate CUSIPs in top-N lists\n3. Validating that increases/decreases make sense\n4. Find Fund AUM (Assets Under Management)\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Search COVERPAGE for fund name to get accession number\n2. Use accession number to query SUMMARYPAGE for AUM\n\n**Code:**\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Step 1: Find accession number\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nmatches = coverpage[coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains(\n    'renaissance technologies', case=False, na=False\n)]\n# Filter out amendments\nmatches = matches[matches['ISAMENDMENT'] != 'Y']\naccession = matches.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n# Step 2: Get AUM\nsummary = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/SUMMARYPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nfund_summary = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession]\naum = fund_summary.iloc[0]['TABLEVALUETOTAL']  # In dollars since Jan 2023\n```\n\n### 2. Count Holdings for a Fund\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Get accession number (as above)\n2. Count rows in INFOTABLE for that accession\n\n**Code:**\n```python\nholdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\nfund_holdings = holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession]\nnum_stocks = len(fund_holdings)\n```\n\n### 3. Compare Holdings Between Quarters\n\n**Purpose:** Find which stocks a fund increased/decreased investment in\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Get accession numbers for both quarters\n2. Load holdings for both quarters\n3. Aggregate by CUSIP (to handle multiple entries per security)\n4. Merge and calculate differences\n5. Sort by change to find top increases/decreases\n\n**Code:**\n```python\n# Get accession numbers\nq2_accession = find_fund_accession('2025-q2/COVERPAGE.tsv', 'Berkshire Hathaway Inc')\nq3_accession = find_fund_accession('2025-q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', 'Berkshire Hathaway Inc')\n\n# Load holdings\nq2_holdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q2/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\nq3_holdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n\n# Filter and aggregate by CUSIP\nq2_fund = q2_holdings[q2_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q2_accession]\nq3_fund = q3_holdings[q3_holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == q3_accession]\n\nq2_agg = q2_fund.groupby('CUSIP').agg({\n    'VALUE': 'sum',\n    'NAMEOFISSUER': 'first'\n}).reset_index()\n\nq3_agg = q3_fund.groupby('CUSIP').agg({\n    'VALUE': 'sum',\n    'NAMEOFISSUER': 'first'\n}).reset_index()\n\n# Merge and calculate change\nmerged = pd.merge(q2_agg, q3_agg, on='CUSIP', how='outer', suffixes=('_q2', '_q3'))\nmerged['VALUE_q2'] = merged['VALUE_q2'].fillna(0)\nmerged['VALUE_q3'] = merged['VALUE_q3'].fillna(0)\nmerged['change'] = merged['VALUE_q3'] - merged['VALUE_q2']\n\n# Top 5 increases\ntop_increases = merged.nlargest(5, 'change')\ntop_cusips = top_increases['CUSIP'].tolist()\n```\n\n**Why Aggregation is Important:**\n- Funds may have multiple entries for the same CUSIP (different classes, options, etc.)\n- Must sum all entries before comparing quarters\n- Without aggregation, you'll get duplicate CUSIPs in results\n\n### 4. Find Top Holders of a Security\n\n**Purpose:** Identify which funds have the largest positions in a stock\n\n**Steps:**\n1. Search INFOTABLE for security name or CUSIP\n2. Sort by VALUE to find top holders\n3. Join with COVERPAGE to get fund manager names\n\n**Code:**\n```python\n# Search for security\nholdings = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\npalantir = holdings[holdings['NAMEOFISSUER'].str.contains(\n    'palantir', case=False, na=False\n)]\n\n# Get top holders\ntop_holders = palantir.nlargest(3, 'VALUE')\n\n# Add fund names\ncoverpage = pd.read_csv('2025-q3/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\nresult = pd.merge(\n    top_holders,\n    coverpage[['ACCESSION_NUMBER', 'FILINGMANAGER_NAME']],\n    on='ACCESSION_NUMBER'\n)\n\nfund_names = result['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].tolist()\n```\n\n## Important Considerations\n\n### Data Quality Issues\n\n1. **Multiple CUSIPs for Same Security**: Some securities may have variations in CUSIP due to data entry errors\n2. **Multiple Entries per CUSIP**: Funds may report the same CUSIP multiple times (different share classes, options)\n3. **Amendments**: Filter out amendment filings (`ISAMENDMENT != 'Y'`) to avoid duplicates\n4. **Missing Values**: Some fields may be NULL or empty\n\n### Performance Tips\n\n1. **Use `low_memory=False`**: Prevents dtype warnings on large files\n2. **Filter Early**: Filter by accession number before loading full datasets\n3. **Aggregate When Needed**: Always aggregate by CUSIP when comparing holdings\n4. **Use Specific Columns**: Select only needed columns to reduce memory usage\n\n### Value Units (CRITICAL)\n\n- **Before January 3, 2023**: Values in thousands of dollars\n- **After January 3, 2023**: Values in dollars\n\nFor Q2 and Q3 2025 data, all VALUE fields are in dollars (not thousands).\n\n## Complete Workflow Example\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\n\ndef analyze_hedge_funds(q2_dir, q3_dir):\n    \"\"\"Complete analysis workflow.\"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n\n    # Q1: Renaissance AUM in Q3\n    coverpage = pd.read_csv(f'{q3_dir}/COVERPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\n    matches = coverpage[\n        (coverpage['FILINGMANAGER_NAME'].str.contains('renaissance', case=False, na=False)) &\n        (coverpage['ISAMENDMENT'] != 'Y')\n    ]\n    accession = matches.iloc[0]['ACCESSION_NUMBER']\n\n    summary = pd.read_csv(f'{q3_dir}/SUMMARYPAGE.tsv', sep='\\t')\n    aum = summary[summary['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession].iloc[0]['TABLEVALUETOTAL']\n    results['q1_answer'] = int(aum)\n\n    # Q2: Count holdings\n    holdings = pd.read_csv(f'{q3_dir}/INFOTABLE.tsv', sep='\\t', low_memory=False)\n    count = len(holdings[holdings['ACCESSION_NUMBER'] == accession])\n    results['q2_answer'] = count\n\n    # Q3: Berkshire increases\n    # (See \"Compare Holdings Between Quarters\" section above)\n\n    # Q4: Palantir top holders\n    # (See \"Find Top Holders of a Security\" section above)\n\n    return results\n\n# Save results\nwith open('answers.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(results, f, indent=4)\n```\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n1. **Not filtering amendments**: Results in duplicate data\n2. **Not aggregating by CUSIP**: Creates cartesian product on merge\n3. **Wrong value units**: Remember values are in dollars (post-2023)\n4. **Case sensitivity**: Always use `case=False` for string searches\n5. **NaN handling**: Fill NaN with 0 before calculations\n\n## Testing Your Results\n\nVerify your analysis by:\n1.
